--- psychedelicraft-1.8.9+1.20.1/fabric.mod.json ---

{
  "schemaVersion": 1,
  "id": "psychedelicraft",
  "version": "1.8.9+1.20.1",
  "name": "Psychedelicraft",
  "description": "Adds drugs into Minecraft. Psychedelicraft specializes on realism and effects. Shaders are being utilized, so keep that in mind.",
  "authors": [
    "Ivorius",
    "TripleHeadedSheep",
    "Sollace"
  ],
  "contact": {
    "homepage": "http://www.minecraftforum.net/topic/563257-152",
    "sources": "https://github.com/Sollace/Psychedelicraft",
    "issues": "https://github.com/Sollace/Psychedelicraft/issues"
  },
  "license": "MIT",
  "icon": "logo.png",
  "accessWidener": "psychedelicraft.aw",
  "environment": "*",
  "entrypoints": {
    "main": [
      "ivorius.psychedelicraft.Psychedelicraft"
    ],
    "client": [
      "ivorius.psychedelicraft.client.PsychedelicraftClient"
    ],
    "fabric-datagen": [
      "ivorius.psychedelicraft.datagen.Datagen"
    ],
    "tla-api": [
      "ivorius.psychedelicraft.compat.tia.Main"
    ],
    "modmenu": [
      "ivorius.psychedelicraft.compat.modmenu.Main"
    ]
  },
  "mixins": [
    "psychedelicraft.mixin.json"
  ],
  "depends": {
    "minecraft": "1.20.1",
    "fabricloader": "\u003e\u003d0.16.14",
    "java": "\u003e\u003d17",
    "fabric-api": "\u003e\u003d0.92.2+1.20.1"
  },
  "suggests": {
    "modmenu": "*"
  },
  "conflicts": {
    "kubejs": "*"
  },
  "custom": {
    "modmenu": {
      "links": {
        "modmenu.discord": "https://discord.gg/mt7uwQ4",
        "modmenu.github_releases": "https://github.com/Sollace/Psychedelicraft/releases"
      }
    }
  },
  "jars": [
    {
      "file": "META-INF/jars/TlaApi-1.2.2-beta.5+1.20.1.jar"
    },
    {
      "file": "META-INF/jars/fabwork-1.2.0.jar"
    },
    {
      "file": "META-INF/jars/kirin-1.15.6+1.20.1.jar"
    },
    {
      "file": "META-INF/jars/terraform-wood-api-v1-7.0.3.jar"
    }
  ]
}

--- psychedelicraft-1.8.9+1.20.1/psychedelicraft.mixin.refmap.json ---

{
  "mappings": {
    "ivorius/psychedelicraft/mixin/MixinAbstractBlockSettings": {
      "offsetter": "field_42818:Ljava/util/Optional;"
    },
    "ivorius/psychedelicraft/mixin/MixinAbstractFurnaceBlockEntity": {
      "net/minecraft/item/ItemStack.decrement(I)V": "Lnet/minecraft/class_1799;method_7934(I)V",
      "tick": "Lnet/minecraft/class_2609;method_31651(Lnet/minecraft/class_1937;Lnet/minecraft/class_2338;Lnet/minecraft/class_2680;Lnet/minecraft/class_2609;)V"
    },

{

      }
    },
    {
      "apply": {
        "model": "psychedelicraft:block/juniper_fence_side",
        "uvlock": true,
        "y": 180
      },
      "when": {
        "south": "true"
      }
    },
    {
      "apply": {
        "model": "psychedelicraft:block/juniper_fence_side",
        "uvlock": true,
        "y": 270
      },
      "when": {
        "west": "true"
      }
    }
  ]
}

        "facing": "north"

        "model": "psychedelicraft:block/distillery_condenser",
        "y": 0
      },
      "when": {
        "facing": "south"
      }
    },
    {
      "apply": {
        "model": "psychedelicraft:block/distillery_condenser",
        "y": 90
      },
      "when": {
        "facing": "west"
      }
    }
  ]
}
